# SECrillion question bank

Generated from `data/questions.json` by `node tools/export-bank.mjs`. Career-fair prompts are left out. Points per tier: Plankton 10 · Schooler 30 · Rare 60 · Deep cut 85 · One in a SECrillion 100.

75 prompts.

## Name a Texas state park or state natural area in TPWD’s directory

`texas-state-parks` · 86 answers · Daily / Unlimited pool · Packs: Aggieland

Scope: All 86 distinct state parks and state natural areas in TPWD’s All Parks directory checked 2026-11-21, including listed sites awaiting opening or with restricted access. Separate units of Cooper Lake, Lake Somerville and Ray Roberts Lake share one answer per park. Standalone historic sites, visitor centers, lodges and tramways are excluded, as are national, city and county parks. The directory’s Cooper State Park entries refer to Cooper Lake State Park.

Sources: [tpwd.texas.gov](https://tpwd.texas.gov/state-parks/nearby/all-parks/), [tpwd.texas.gov](https://tpwd.texas.gov/state-parks/cooper-lake)

## Name an official SI metric prefix (use the name)

`si-prefixes` · 24 answers · Daily / Unlimited pool · Packs: Engineering Life, Lab Rats

Scope: All 24 official SI prefixes, including the four adopted in 2022. Name answers only: symbol-only aliases would collide in the game’s case-insensitive matcher (M/m, P/p, Y/y, etc.). Binary prefixes such as kibi are excluded.

Sources: [www.nist.gov](https://www.nist.gov/pml/owm/metric-si-prefixes), [www.bipm.org](https://www.bipm.org/en/measurement-units/si-prefixes)

**Deep cut (85) · 10:** Peta; Exa; Zetta; Yotta; Femto; Atto; Zepto; Yocto; Ronna; Quetta

**One in a SECrillion (100) · 2:** Ronto; Quecto

## Name a street on the Texas A&M campus

`cstat-streets` · 128 answers · Daily / Unlimited pool · Packs: Aggieland

## Name a restaurant or eatery within College Station city limits with at least 50 Google reviews

`cstat-eats` · 270 answers · Daily / Unlimited pool · Packs: Campus Life

Scope: Currently operating restaurants, fast food, cafes, tea/coffee shops, bakeries, dessert shops, food trucks, dining halls, and food-serving bars and dine-in cinemas within College Station city limits. At least one College Station location must have 50 or more Google reviews on its own listing; do not add reviews across locations or count reviews for a hotel, hospital, grocery store, or other host facility. Multiple locations of the same eatery count as one answer. Bryan and unincorporated postal College Station addresses do not count. Eligibility is the 2026-11-21 research snapshot; review counts are not updated live. Refresh sources when businesses open, close, move, or cross the threshold.

## Name a Boolean logic-gate family with up to two inputs and one output

`logic-gates` · 12 answers · Packs: Lab Rats, Code & Cosmos

Scope: All 16 Boolean functions of two variables, grouped into 12 gate families: the six symmetric binary gates, buffer/projection, NOT, constant true, constant false, implication and nonimplication. Exchanging input names does not create another family. Quantum gates, multi-output gates, tri-state/non-Boolean outputs and three-or-more-input functions are excluded.

Sources: [www.hyperphysics.phy-astr.gsu.edu](https://www.hyperphysics.phy-astr.gsu.edu/hbase/Electronic/diglog.html), [reference.wolfram.com](https://reference.wolfram.com/language/ref/BooleanFunction.html)

**Plankton (10) · 2:** AND; OR

**Schooler (30) · 1:** NOT (Inverter)

**Rare (60) · 5:** NAND; XOR (Exclusive OR); NOR; Constant true (TRUE, Always true, Tautology, Logical one, Tie high); Constant false (FALSE, Always false, Contradiction, Logical zero, Tie low)

**Deep cut (85) · 3:** XNOR (Exclusive NOR); Implication (IMPLIES, Material implication, Converse implication, IMPLY); Nonimplication (NIMPLIES, Non implication, Non-implication, Converse nonimplication, NIMPLY)

**One in a SECrillion (100) · 1:** Buffer

## Name a renewable source or technology in the EIA/DOE renewable-energy reference list

`renewable-energy` · 23 answers · Packs: Build It

Scope: The five EIA main renewable-source categories, their explicitly named biomass fuels, and marine/solar technologies in the published reference list, alongside the original accepted source families. Fossil fuels, conventional nuclear power, ordinary batteries and hydrogen without a specified renewable production pathway are excluded. Related source and technology answers may count separately.

Sources: [www.eia.gov](https://www.eia.gov/energyexplained/renewable-sources/types-and-usage.php), [www.energy.gov](https://www.energy.gov/eere/water/marine-energy-basics)

**Plankton (10) · 2:** Solar (Solar power, Sun); Wind (Wind power)

**Schooler (30) · 1:** Hydroelectric (Hydro, Hydropower, Water)

**Rare (60) · 2:** Geothermal; Tidal

**Deep cut (85) · 16:** Biomass (Wood, Wood waste); Wave; Biofuel (Biodiesel, Ethanol, Bioethanol); Biogas (Landfill gas); Municipal solid waste (MSW, Biogenic municipal waste); Sewage gas (Wastewater biogas); Agricultural waste (Crop waste); Renewable diesel; Renewable jet fuel (Sustainable aviation fuel, SAF); Biobutanol; Marine energy (Ocean energy); Ocean current energy (Ocean currents); River current energy (River currents, Hydrokinetic); Concentrated solar power (Concentrating solar power, CSP, Solar thermal); Solar photovoltaic (Photovoltaic, PV); Run of river hydropower (Run-of-river)

**One in a SECrillion (100) · 2:** Ocean thermal (OTEC); Osmotic (Salinity gradient, Blue energy, Salinity gradient power)

## Name a metallic element in PubChem’s periodic table or an alloy in the metals reference list

`metals` · 126 answers · Packs: Lab Rats, Build It

Scope: All elements in PubChem’s Alkali metal, Alkaline earth metal, Transition metal, Post-transition metal, Lanthanide and Actinide groups as retrieved 2026-11-23, plus the originally accepted alloy families. Metalloids/nonmetals and arbitrary commercial grades are excluded. Element symbols are accepted only when unambiguous.

Sources: [pubchem.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov](https://pubchem.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/rest/pug/periodictable/JSON), [periodic-table.rsc.org](https://periodic-table.rsc.org/)
